   232  fn applyResponseTimeout(stream: std.Io.net.Stream) void {
   233      // A receive deadline is a POSIX socket option that `std.posix` does not
   234      // expose on Windows. The wait this bounds is short and the caller also holds
   235      // a deadline, so on Windows the socket is left as it is.
   236      if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag == .windows) return;
   237      std.posix.setsockopt(
   238          stream.socket.handle,
   239          std.posix.SOL.SOCKET,
   240          std.posix.SO.RCVTIMEO,
   241          std.mem.asBytes(&response_timeout),
   242      ) catch {};
   243  }
   244  
   245| =======
   246  >>>>>>> origin/main
   247  fn clampStatus(custom_status: ?[]const u8) ?[]const u8 {
   248      const status = custom_status orelse return null;
   249      if (status.len == 0) return null;
   250      return status[0..@min(status.len, custom_status_max)];
   251  }

  1536  fn traceFilePermissions() std.Io.File.Permissions {
  1537      const builtin = @import("builtin");
  1538      return switch (builtin.target.os.tag) {
  1539          .windows => .default_file,
  1540          else => io_mod.permissionsForMode(0o600),
  1541      };
  1542  }

  1544  fn writeTraceReportFile(alloc: std.mem.Allocator, contents: []const u8) ![]u8 {
  1545      const tmp_dir = io_mod.getenv("TMPDIR") orelse "/tmp";
  1546      const trimmed = std.mem.trimEnd(u8, tmp_dir, "/");
  1547      const now_ms = io_mod.milliTimestamp();
  1548      const now_secs: i64 = @max(@divFloor(now_ms, 1000), 0);
  1549      const epoch_secs: std.time.epoch.EpochSeconds = .{ .secs = @intCast(now_secs) };
  1550      const day_seconds = epoch_secs.getDaySeconds();
  1551      const year_day = epoch_secs.getEpochDay().calculateYearDay();
  1552      const month_day = year_day.calculateMonthDay();
  1553      var attempts: u8 = 0;
  1554      while (attempts < 8) : (attempts += 1) {
  1555          var random_bytes: [6]u8 = undefined;
  1556          io_mod.getIo().random(&random_bytes);
  1557          const random_hex = std.fmt.bytesToHex(random_bytes, .lower);
  1558          const path = try alloc.print("{s}/fx-trace-{d}-{d:0>2}-{d:0>2}-{d:0>2}{d:0>2}{d:0>2}-{s}.md", .{
  1559              trimmed,
  1560              year_day.year,
  1561              month_day.month.numeric(),
  1562              month_day.day_index + 1,
  1563              day_seconds.getHoursIntoDay(),
  1564              day_seconds.getMinutesIntoHour(),
  1565              day_seconds.getSecondsIntoMinute(),
  1566              random_hex,
  1567          });
  1568          errdefer alloc.free(path);
  1569  
  1570          var file = std.Io.Dir.createFileAbsolute(io_mod.getIo(), path, .{
  1571              .truncate = false,
  1572              .exclusive = true,
  1573              .permissions = traceFilePermissions(),
  1574          }) catch |err| switch (err) {
  1575              error.PathAlreadyExists => {
  1576                  alloc.free(path);
  1577                  continue;
  1578              },
  1579              else => return err,
  1580          };
  1581          defer file.close(io_mod.getIo());
  1582          try file.writeStreamingAll(io_mod.getIo(), contents);
  1583          try file.sync(io_mod.getIo());
  1584  
  1585          return path;
  1586      }
  1587      return error.PathAlreadyExists;
  1588  }

  1590  fn buildTraceReport(app: anytype) ![]u8 {
  1591      const App = @TypeOf(app.*);
  1592      const builtin = @import("builtin");
  1593  
  1594      var out: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = .init(app.alloc);
  1595      defer out.deinit();
  1596  
  1597      try out.writer.writeAll("# fx trace\n\n");
  1598      try out.writer.writeAll("Private diagnostic report. It may include prompts, file paths, command output, and file snippets.\n\n");
  1599  
  1600      try out.writer.writeAll("## Summary\n");
  1601      const now_secs: i64 = @divFloor(io_mod.milliTimestamp(), 1000);
  1602      if (now_secs >= 0) {

  1604          const day_seconds = epoch_secs.getDaySeconds();

  1612              day_seconds.getMinutesIntoHour(),
  1613              day_seconds.getSecondsIntoMinute(),

  1615      }

  1629      try out.writer.print("permission_mode: {s}\n", .{perm_label});
  1630      try out.writer.print("workspace: {s}\n", .{app.workspace_root});
  1631  
  1632      try writeCurrentStateSummary(&out.writer, app, app.alloc);
  1633      try writeProblemsSummary(&out.writer, app, app.alloc);
  1634      try writeLastShutdownSection(&out.writer, app.alloc);
  1635      try writeCompactionSummary(&out.writer, app.alloc);
  1636      try writeLastInterruptedDetail(&out.writer, agents.screenAgentOf(app).session.agent.history.items, app.alloc);
  1637      try writeSessionTitleSummary(&out.writer, app, app.alloc);

  1660  }

  1662  noinline fn writeMaskedInline(writer: *std.Io.Writer, alloc: std.mem.Allocator, text: []const u8) !void {
  1663      const masked = try text_utils.maskSecrets(alloc, text);
  1664      defer if (masked.ptr != text.ptr) alloc.free(masked);
  1665      try writer.writeAll(masked);
  1666  }
  1667  
  1668  fn traceToolDisplayName(tool_name: []const u8) []const u8 {
  1669      return if (tool_presentation.isProviderSearchAlias(tool_name))
  1670          "web_search"
  1671      else
  1672          tool_name;
  1673  }
  1674  
  1675  fn isTraceToolTokenByte(byte: u8) bool {
  1676      return std.ascii.isAlphanumeric(byte) or byte == '_';
  1677  }
  1678  
  1679  fn providerSearchAliasPrefixLen(token: []const u8) ?usize {
  1680      var end: usize = 1;
  1681      while (end <= token.len) : (end += 1) {
  1682          if (!tool_presentation.isProviderSearchAlias(token[0..end])) continue;
  1683          if (end == token.len or token[end] == '_') return end;
  1684      }
  1685      return null;
  1686  }
  1687  
  1688  fn writeTraceTextNeutralized(writer: *std.Io.Writer, text: []const u8) !void {
  1689      var token_start: usize = 0;
  1690      var scan_index: usize = 0;
  1691      var written_through: usize = 0;
  1692      while (scan_index < text.len) {
  1693          if (!isTraceToolTokenByte(text[scan_index])) {
  1694              scan_index += 1;
  1695              continue;
  1696          }
  1697          token_start = scan_index;
  1698          while (scan_index < text.len and isTraceToolTokenByte(text[scan_index])) : (scan_index += 1) {}
  1699          const alias_len = providerSearchAliasPrefixLen(text[token_start..scan_index]) orelse continue;
  1700          try writer.writeAll(text[written_through..token_start]);
  1701          try writer.writeAll("web_search");
  1702          written_through = token_start + alias_len;
  1703      }
  1704      try writer.writeAll(text[written_through..]);
  1705  }
  1706  
  1707  fn writeCurrentStateSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer, app: anytype, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  1708      const App = @TypeOf(app.*);
  1709      try writer.writeAll("\n## Current State\n");
  1710      if (comptime agents.hasAgentField(App, "session_persistence")) {
  1711          if (app_session_runtime.Runtime(App).activeSessionId(agents.screenAgentOf(app))) |id| {
  1712              try writer.print("session_id: {s}\n", .{id});
  1713          }
  1714          const session_dir =
  1715              try app_session_runtime.Runtime(App).activeSessionDisplayPath(
  1716                  agents.screenAgentOf(app),
  1717                  alloc,
  1718              );
  1719          if (session_dir) |dir| {
  1720              defer alloc.free(dir);
  1721              try writer.print("session_dir: {s}\n", .{dir});
  1722          }
  1723      }
  1724      if (comptime @hasField(App, "agent_step_limit")) try writer.print("agent_step_limit: {d}\n", .{app.agent_step_limit});
  1725      if (comptime agents.hasAgentField(App, "effort")) try writer.print("effort: {s}\n", .{agents.screenAgentOf(app).effort.label()});
  1726      if (comptime agents.hasAgentField(App, "total_input_tokens") and agents.hasAgentField(App, "total_output_tokens")) {
  1727          try writer.print("tokens_total: {d}->{d}\n", .{ agents.loadTotal(&agents.screenAgentOf(app).total_input_tokens), agents.loadTotal(&agents.screenAgentOf(app).total_output_tokens) });
  1728      }
  1729      if (comptime agents.hasAgentField(App, "total_web_search_requests")) {
  1730          try writer.print("web_search_requests_total: {d}\n", .{agents.loadTotal(&agents.screenAgentOf(app).total_web_search_requests)});
  1731      }
  1732      try writeAuthStateSummary(writer, app);
  1733      try writeProcessSummary(writer, alloc);
  1734      if (comptime agents.hasAgentField(App, "worker")) {
  1735          const snapshot = agents.screenAgentOf(app).worker.snapshotState(alloc) catch null;
  1736          if (snapshot) |state| {
  1737              defer state.deinit(alloc);
  1738              try writer.print("worker: processing={s} queued={d} cancel={s}\n", .{ boolLabel(state.processing), state.queued_count, boolLabel(state.cancel_requested) });
  1739              if (state.pending_permission_request) |request| {
  1740                  try writer.writeAll("pending_permission: ");
  1741                  try writeMaskedInline(writer, alloc, request.label);
  1742                  try writer.writeByte('\n');
  1743              }
  1744          }
  1745          const pending_questions = agents.screenAgentOf(app).worker.snapshotPendingQuestionBatch(alloc) catch null;
  1746          if (pending_questions) |questions| {
  1747              defer questions.deinit(alloc);
  1748              try writer.print("pending_questions: {d}\n", .{questions.entries.len});
  1749              for (questions.entries, 0..) |entry, idx| {
  1750                  if (idx >= 3) {
  1751                      try writer.print("  ... {d} more pending questions\n", .{questions.entries.len - idx});
  1752                      break;
  1753                  }
  1754                  try writer.writeAll("  - ");
  1755                  try writeMaskedInline(writer, alloc, entry.question);
  1756                  try writer.print(" options={d}\n", .{entry.options.len});
  1757              }
  1758          }
  1759      }
  1760      if (comptime agents.hasAgentField(App, "pending_images")) {
  1761          if (agents.screenAgentOf(app).pending_images.items.len > 0) {
  1762              try writer.print("pending_images: {d}\n", .{agents.screenAgentOf(app).pending_images.items.len});
  1763              for (agents.screenAgentOf(app).pending_images.items, 0..) |image, idx| {
  1764                  if (idx >= 3) {
  1765                      try writer.print("  ... {d} more pending images\n", .{agents.screenAgentOf(app).pending_images.items.len - idx});
  1766                      break;
  1767                  }
  1768                  try writer.print("  - media={s} path=", .{image.media_type});
  1769                  try writeMaskedInline(writer, alloc, image.path);
  1770                  try writer.writeByte('\n');
  1771              }
  1772          }
  1773      }
  1774      if (comptime @hasField(App, "context_enabled") and agents.hasAgentField(App, "context_snapshot")) {
  1775          try writer.print("project_context: enabled={s}", .{boolLabel(app.context_enabled)});
  1776          const context_bytes = agents.screenAgentOf(app).context_snapshot.modelVisibleBytes();
  1777          if (context_bytes.len > 0) try writer.print(" bytes={d}", .{context_bytes.len}) else try writer.writeAll(" not_loaded");
  1778          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  1779      }
  1780      try writeDebugEnvSummary(writer, alloc);
  1781      if (comptime agents.hasAgentField(App, "stream")) {
  1782          try writer.print("stream: active={s} chunks={d} reads={d} lists={d} writes={d} edits={d} commands={d} subagents={d}", .{
  1783              boolLabel(agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.active),
  1784              agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.chunks,
  1785              agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.read_count,
  1786              agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.list_count,
  1787              agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.write_count,
  1788              agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.edit_count,
  1789              agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.command_count,
  1790              agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.subagent_count,
  1791          });
  1792          if (agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.last_activity_kind) |kind| try writer.print(" last_activity={s}", .{@tagName(kind)});
  1793          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  1794      }
  1795      try writer.print("terminal: {d} cols x {d} rows\n", .{ app.shell.layout.cols, app.shell.layout.rows });
  1796      try writer.print("metrics: ansi_bytes={d} full_redraws={d} debounced_resizes={d} footer_updates={d} stream_chunks={d}\n", .{
  1797          app.metrics.ansi_bytes,
  1798          app.metrics.full_redraws,
  1799          app.metrics.debounced_resizes,
  1800          app.metrics.footer_line_updates,
  1801          app.metrics.stream_chunks,
  1802      });
  1803  }
  1804  
  1805  fn writeProcessSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  1806      const pid_number = io_mod.currentProcessId();
  1807      try writer.print("process: pid={d}", .{pid_number});
  1808      if (countOpenFileDescriptors()) |fd_count| try writer.print(" open_fds={d}", .{fd_count});
  1809      try writer.writeByte('\n');
  1810  
  1811      const ps = processMemorySnapshot(alloc, io_mod.pidFromNumber(@truncate(pid_number))) catch null;
  1812      if (ps) |text| {
  1813          defer alloc.free(text);
  1814          const trimmed = std.mem.trim(u8, text, " \t\r\n");
  1815          if (trimmed.len > 0) {
  1816              try writer.writeAll("process_memory:\n");
  1817              var it = std.mem.splitScalar(u8, trimmed, '\n');
  1818              while (it.next()) |line| {
  1819                  try writer.writeAll("  ");
  1820                  try writer.writeAll(std.mem.trimEnd(u8, line, " \t\r"));
  1821                  try writer.writeByte('\n');
  1822              }
  1823          }
  1824      }
  1825  }
  1826  
  1827  fn countOpenFileDescriptors() ?usize {
  1828      const builtin = @import("builtin");
  1829      const fd_dir = switch (builtin.target.os.tag) {
  1830          .linux => "/proc/self/fd",
  1831          .macos => "/dev/fd",
  1832          else => return null,
  1833      };
  1834      var dir = std.Io.Dir.openDirAbsolute(io_mod.getIo(), fd_dir, .{ .iterate = true }) catch return null;
  1835      defer dir.close(io_mod.getIo());
  1836  
  1837      var count: usize = 0;
  1838      var it = dir.iterate();
  1839      while (it.next(io_mod.getIo()) catch return null) |entry| {
  1840          if (std.mem.eql(u8, entry.name, ".") or std.mem.eql(u8, entry.name, "..")) continue;
  1841          count += 1;
  1842      }
  1843      return count;
  1844  }
  1845  
  1846  /// A one-line memory and lifetime summary for the running process.
  1847  ///
  1848  /// POSIX asks `ps`, which every POSIX host has. Windows has no `ps`, so it reads
  1849  /// the same facts from the kernel through `GetProcessMemoryInfo` and
  1850  /// `GetProcessTimes`, which is what a Windows tool would do.
  1851  fn processMemorySnapshot(alloc: std.mem.Allocator, pid: std.posix.pid_t) ![]u8 {
  1852      if (comptime @import("builtin").target.os.tag == .windows) return windowsMemorySnapshot(alloc);
  1853      const pid_text = try alloc.print("{d}", .{pid});
  1854      defer alloc.free(pid_text);
  1855      const result = try std.process.run(alloc, io_mod.getIo(), .{
  1856          .argv = &.{ "ps", "-o", "pid,ppid,rss,vsz,etime,stat", "-p", pid_text },
  1857      });
  1858      defer alloc.free(result.stderr);
  1859      if (result.term != .exited or result.term.exited != 0) {
  1860          alloc.free(result.stdout);
  1861          return error.ProcessSnapshotFailed;
  1862      }
  1863      return result.stdout;
  1864  }
  1865  
  1866  /// Reads this process's memory counters and elapsed time from the kernel, in the
  1867  /// same shape `ps` reports them on POSIX.
  1868  ///
  1869  /// `ps` on Windows prints PowerShell process objects, which is not the same data
  1870  /// and not present on every install, so the kernel counters are used instead.
  1871  fn windowsMemorySnapshot(alloc: std.mem.Allocator) ![]u8 {
  1872      var counters: windows_memory.PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS = std.mem.zeroes(windows_memory.PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS);
  1873      counters.cb = @sizeOf(windows_memory.PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS);
  1874      if (windows_memory.GetProcessMemoryInfo(
  1875          windows_memory.current_process,
  1876          &counters,
  1877          counters.cb,
  1878      ) == 0) return error.ProcessSnapshotFailed;
  1879  
  1880      var creation: windows_memory.FILETIME = undefined;
  1881      var exit_time: windows_memory.FILETIME = undefined;
  1882      var kernel_time: windows_memory.FILETIME = undefined;
  1883      var user_time: windows_memory.FILETIME = undefined;
  1884      var elapsed_text: [32]u8 = undefined;
  1885      const elapsed: ?[]const u8 = if (windows_memory.GetProcessTimes(
  1886          windows_memory.current_process,
  1887          &creation,
  1888          &exit_time,
  1889          &kernel_time,
  1890          &user_time,
  1891      ) != 0)
  1892          windowsElapsed(creation, &elapsed_text)
  1893      else
  1894          null;
  1895  
  1896      const rss_kb = counters.WorkingSetSize / 1024;
  1897      const vsz_kb = counters.PagefileUsage / 1024;
  1898      return if (elapsed) |text|
  1899          alloc.print("pid={d} rss_kb={d} vsz_kb={d} etime={s}\n", .{
  1900              io_mod.currentProcessId(),
  1901              rss_kb,
  1902              vsz_kb,
  1903              text,
  1904          })
  1905      else
  1906          alloc.print("pid={d} rss_kb={d} vsz_kb={d}\n", .{
  1907              io_mod.currentProcessId(),
  1908              rss_kb,
  1909              vsz_kb,
  1910          });
  1911  }
  1912  
  1913  /// Formats a process's wall-clock age as `HH:MM:SS`, the shape `ps` uses.
  1914  fn windowsElapsed(creation: windows_memory.FILETIME, buffer: *[32]u8) ?[]const u8 {
  1915      const created_ticks = (@as(u64, creation.dwHighDateTime) << 32) | creation.dwLowDateTime;
  1916      if (created_ticks == 0) return null;
  1917      var now: windows_memory.FILETIME = undefined;
  1918      windows_memory.GetSystemTimeAsFileTime(&now);
  1919      const now_ticks = (@as(u64, now.dwHighDateTime) << 32) | now.dwLowDateTime;
  1920      if (now_ticks < created_ticks) return null;
  1921      // FILETIME counts 100-nanosecond intervals.
  1922      const elapsed_seconds = (now_ticks - created_ticks) / 10_000_000;
  1923      const hours = elapsed_seconds / 3600;
  1924      const minutes = (elapsed_seconds % 3600) / 60;
  1925      const seconds = elapsed_seconds % 60;
  1926      return std.mem.print(buffer, "{d:0>2}:{d:0>2}:{d:0>2}", .{ hours, minutes, seconds }) catch null;
  1927  }
  1928  
  1929  const windows_memory = if (@import("builtin").target.os.tag != .windows) struct {} else struct {
  1930      const FILETIME = extern struct {
  1931          dwLowDateTime: u32,
  1932          dwHighDateTime: u32,
  1933      };
  1934      const PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS = extern struct {
  1935          cb: u32,
  1936          PageFaultCount: u32,
  1937          PeakWorkingSetSize: usize,
  1938          WorkingSetSize: usize,
  1939          QuotaPeakPagedPoolUsage: usize,
  1940          QuotaPagedPoolUsage: usize,
  1941          QuotaPeakNonPagedPoolUsage: usize,
  1942          QuotaNonPagedPoolUsage: usize,
  1943          PagefileUsage: usize,
  1944          PeakPagefileUsage: usize,
  1945      };
  1946      /// The pseudo-handle `GetCurrentProcess` returns, `(HANDLE)-1`.
  1947      const current_process: std.posix.fd_t = @ptrFromInt(std.math.maxInt(usize));
  1948  
  1949      extern "kernel32" fn GetCurrentProcess() callconv(.winapi) std.posix.fd_t;
  1950      extern "kernel32" fn GetProcessTimes(
  1951          process: std.posix.fd_t,
  1952          creation_time: *FILETIME,
  1953          exit_time: *FILETIME,
  1954          kernel_time: *FILETIME,
  1955          user_time: *FILETIME,
  1956      ) callconv(.winapi) c_int;
  1957      extern "kernel32" fn GetSystemTimeAsFileTime(file_time: *FILETIME) callconv(.winapi) void;
  1958      extern "psapi" fn GetProcessMemoryInfo(
  1959          process: std.posix.fd_t,
  1960          counters: *PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS,
  1961          counters_len: u32,
  1962      ) callconv(.winapi) c_int;
  1963  };

  1965  fn writeDebugEnvSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  1966      try writer.print("FX_TRACE: {s}\n", .{if (envTruthy("FX_TRACE")) "on" else "off"});
  1967      if (debug_trace.activeLogPath() orelse io_mod.getenv("FX_TRACE_LOG")) |path| {
  1968          try writer.writeAll("trace_log: ");
  1969          try writeMaskedInline(writer, alloc, path);
  1970          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  1971      }
  1972  }
  1973  
  1974  fn envTruthy(name: []const u8) bool {
  1975      const raw = io_mod.getenv(name) orelse return false;
  1976      const trimmed = std.mem.trim(u8, raw, " \t\r\n");
  1977      return std.ascii.eqlIgnoreCase(trimmed, "1") or
  1978          std.ascii.eqlIgnoreCase(trimmed, "true") or
  1979          std.ascii.eqlIgnoreCase(trimmed, "yes") or
  1980          std.ascii.eqlIgnoreCase(trimmed, "on");
  1981  }

  1983  fn writeAuthStateSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer, app: anytype) !void {
  1984      const App = @TypeOf(app.*);
  1985      if (comptime !@hasField(App, "auth")) return;
  1986  
  1987      const auth_view = app.auth.view();
  1988      try writer.print(
  1989          "auth: source={s} refreshable={s} gateway_team={s}\n",
  1990          .{
  1991              auth_view.activeSourceLabel(),
  1992              boolLabel(auth_view.refreshable),
  1993              auth_view.gatewayTeamStatus().label(),
  1994          },
  1995      );
  1996  }
  1997  
  1998  fn writeLastShutdownSection(writer: *std.Io.Writer, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  1999      try writer.writeAll("\n## Last Shutdown\n");
  2000      const contents = app_lifecycle.readLastShutdownReport(alloc) orelse {
  2001          try writer.writeAll("(none recorded)\n");
  2002          return;
  2003      };
  2004      defer alloc.free(contents);
  2005      const parsed = std.json.parseFromSlice(std.json.Value, alloc, contents, .{}) catch {
  2006          try writer.writeAll("(last shutdown report unreadable)\n");
  2007          return;
  2008      };
  2009      defer parsed.deinit();
  2010      const root = parsed.value.object;
  2011      const total_ms = if (root.get("total_ms")) |value| value.integer else -1;
  2012      try writer.print("total_ms={d}", .{total_ms});
  2013      if (root.get("recorded_at_ms")) |value| {
  2014          if (value.integer >= 0) {
  2015              const epoch_secs: std.time.epoch.EpochSeconds = .{ .secs = @intCast(@divFloor(value.integer, 1000)) };
  2016              const day_seconds = epoch_secs.getDaySeconds();
  2017              const year_day = epoch_secs.getEpochDay().calculateYearDay();
  2018              const month_day = year_day.calculateMonthDay();
  2019              try writer.print(" recorded={d}-{d:0>2}-{d:0>2}T{d:0>2}:{d:0>2}:{d:0>2}Z", .{
  2020                  year_day.year,
  2021                  month_day.month.numeric(),
  2022                  month_day.day_index + 1,
  2023                  day_seconds.getHoursIntoDay(),
  2024                  day_seconds.getMinutesIntoHour(),
  2025                  day_seconds.getSecondsIntoMinute(),
  2026              });
  2027          }
  2028      }
  2029      try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2030      if (root.get("stages")) |stages_value| {
  2031          for (stages_value.array.items) |stage_value| {
  2032              const stage = stage_value.object;
  2033              const name = if (stage.get("name")) |v| v.string else "?";
  2034              const step_ms = if (stage.get("step_ms")) |v| v.integer else -1;
  2035              try writer.print("- {s} step_ms={d}\n", .{ name, step_ms });
  2036          }
  2037      }
  2038  }
  2039  
  2040  fn writeProblemsSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer, app: anytype, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  2041      const App = @TypeOf(app.*);
  2042      try writer.writeAll("\n## Problems\n");
  2043      var count: usize = 0;
  2044  
  2045      const state = agents.screenAgentOf(app).worker.snapshotState(alloc) catch null;
  2046      if (state) |snapshot| {
  2047          defer snapshot.deinit(alloc);
  2048          if (snapshot.processing or agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.active) {
  2049              count += 1;
  2050              try writer.print("- report captured an active turn; state may be partial processing={s} stream_active={s} queued={d}\n", .{ boolLabel(snapshot.processing), boolLabel(agents.screenAgentOf(app).stream.active), snapshot.queued_count });
  2051          }
  2052      }
  2053  
  2054      if (lastInterruptedTurn(agents.screenAgentOf(app).session.agent.history.items)) |entry| {
  2055          count += 1;
  2056          try writer.writeAll("- interrupted turn");
  2057          if (entry.tool_call) |call| try writer.print(" in_flight_tool={s}", .{traceToolDisplayName(call.name)});
  2058          if (entry.completed_tool_names.len > 0) try writer.print(" completed_tools={d}", .{entry.completed_tool_names.len});
  2059          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2060      }
  2061  
  2062      if (comptime agents.hasAgentField(App, "session_persistence")) {
  2063          const Persistence = @TypeOf(agents.screenAgentOf(app).session_persistence);
  2064          if (comptime @hasField(Persistence, "title_generation")) {
  2065              const TitleGeneration = @TypeOf(agents.screenAgentOf(app).session_persistence.title_generation);
  2066              if (comptime @hasField(TitleGeneration, "last")) {
  2067                  const last = &agents.screenAgentOf(app).session_persistence.title_generation.last;
  2068                  if (last.status == .failed or last.status == .dropped) {
  2069                      count += 1;
  2070                      try writer.print("- session title generation {s}", .{@tagName(last.status)});
  2071                      if (last.reason) |reason| try writer.print(" reason={s}", .{@tagName(reason)});
  2072                      if (last.detail.len > 0) try writer.print(" detail={s}", .{last.detail});
  2073                      try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2074                  }
  2075              }
  2076          }
  2077      }
  2078  
  2079      var network_buf: [diagnostics.network_ring_capacity]diagnostics.NetworkCall = undefined;
  2080      const network_n = diagnostics.snapshotNetworkCalls(&network_buf);
  2081      var network_reported: usize = 0;
  2082      var ni = network_n;
  2083      while (ni > 0 and network_reported < 3) {
  2084          ni -= 1;
  2085          const call = network_buf[ni];
  2086          if (!networkCallIsError(call)) continue;
  2087          count += 1;
  2088          network_reported += 1;
  2089          try writer.writeAll("- network ");
  2090          try writeNetworkCallCompact(writer, call);
  2091      }
  2092  
  2093      var tool_buf: [diagnostics.tool_call_ring_capacity]diagnostics.ToolCallMetric = undefined;
  2094      const tool_n = diagnostics.snapshotToolCalls(&tool_buf);
  2095      var tool_reported: usize = 0;
  2096      var ti = tool_n;
  2097      while (ti > 0 and tool_reported < 5) {
  2098          ti -= 1;
  2099          const call = tool_buf[ti];
  2100          if (call.outcome == .succeeded) continue;
  2101          count += 1;
  2102          tool_reported += 1;
  2103          try writer.writeAll("- tool ");
  2104          try writeToolCallCompact(writer, call);
  2105      }
  2106  
  2107      var compaction_buf: [diagnostics.compaction_ring_capacity]diagnostics.CompactionEvent = undefined;
  2108      const compaction_n = diagnostics.snapshotCompactionEvents(&compaction_buf);
  2109      var compaction_reported: usize = 0;
  2110      var ci = compaction_n;
  2111      while (ci > 0 and compaction_reported < 3) {
  2112          ci -= 1;
  2113          const event = &compaction_buf[ci];
  2114          if (!event.failed) continue;
  2115          count += 1;
  2116          compaction_reported += 1;
  2117          try writer.writeAll("- context compaction ");
  2118          try writer.writeAll(event.name());
  2119          if (event.turn_id != 0) try writer.print(" turn_id={d}", .{event.turn_id});
  2120          if (event.detail_len > 0) {
  2121              try writer.writeAll(" detail=");
  2122              const detail = event.detail();
  2123              const visible = if (detail.len > 160) detail[0..160] else detail;
  2124              try writeTraceTextNeutralized(writer, visible);
  2125              if (detail.len > 160) try writer.writeAll(" ...");
  2126          }
  2127          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2128      }
  2129  
  2130      count += try writeMcpHostProblems(writer, app, alloc);
  2131  
  2132      count += try writeModelCatalogProblems(writer);
  2133  
  2134      if (count == 0) try writer.writeAll("- no obvious errors captured in recent network, tool, compaction, MCP, or model catalog state\n");
  2135  }
  2136  
  2137  /// Surfaces failed model-catalog loads, capability lookup misses, and image
  2138  /// gate rejections under Problems so an unverifiable-capability failure is
  2139  /// never reported as "no obvious errors".
  2140  fn writeModelCatalogProblems(writer: *std.Io.Writer) !usize {
  2141      var buf: [diagnostics.model_catalog_ring_capacity]diagnostics.ModelCatalogEvent = undefined;
  2142      const total = diagnostics.snapshotModelCatalogEvents(&buf);
  2143      var count: usize = 0;
  2144      var index = total;
  2145      while (index > 0 and count < 3) {
  2146          index -= 1;
  2147          const event = &buf[index];
  2148          if (!event.failed) continue;
  2149          count += 1;
  2150          try writer.print("- model catalog {s}", .{event.name()});
  2151          if (event.detail().len > 0) {
  2152              try writer.writeByte(' ');
  2153              try writer.writeAll(event.detail());
  2154          }
  2155          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2156      }
  2157      return count;
  2158  }
  2159  
  2160  const trace_compaction_max_events: usize = 24;
  2161  
  2162  /// Renders the always-on compaction decision and failure trail so a shared
  2163  /// trace explains what the compaction pipeline did even when FX_TRACE was off.
  2164  fn writeCompactionSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  2165      var buf: [diagnostics.compaction_ring_capacity]diagnostics.CompactionEvent = undefined;
  2166      const total = diagnostics.snapshotCompactionEvents(&buf);
  2167  
  2168      try writer.writeAll("\n## Context Compaction\n");
  2169      if (total == 0) {
  2170          try writer.writeAll("(none recorded)\n");
  2171          return;
  2172      }
  2173      var failed: usize = 0;
  2174      for (buf[0..total]) |event| {
  2175          if (event.failed) failed += 1;
  2176      }
  2177      try writer.print("last={d} failed={d}", .{ total, failed });
  2178      // Events evicted by the bounded ring are reported, not silently dropped.
  2179      const overwritten = buf[0].sequence -| 1;
  2180      if (overwritten > 0) try writer.print(" overwritten_before={d}", .{overwritten});
  2181      try writer.writeAll(" (always recorded; does not require FX_TRACE)\n");
  2182  
  2183      const start = if (total > trace_compaction_max_events) total - trace_compaction_max_events else 0;
  2184      if (start > 0) try writer.print("... ({d} older events omitted)\n", .{start});
  2185      for (buf[start..total]) |*event| {
  2186          var line: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = .init(alloc);
  2187          defer line.deinit();
  2188          try writeTraceTimestampUtc(&line.writer, event.timestamp_ms);
  2189          try line.writer.print(" event={s}", .{event.name()});
  2190          if (event.turn_id != 0) try line.writer.print(" turn_id={d}", .{event.turn_id});
  2191          if (event.step_id != 0) try line.writer.print(" step_id={d}", .{event.step_id});
  2192          if (event.subagent_id != 0) try line.writer.print(" subagent_id={d}", .{event.subagent_id});
  2193          if (event.failed) try line.writer.writeAll(" failed");
  2194          if (event.detail_len > 0) {
  2195              try line.writer.writeByte(' ');
  2196              try line.writer.writeAll(event.detail());
  2197          }
  2198          if (event.truncated) try line.writer.writeAll(" ...");
  2199          // Compaction events carry internal counters and enum names only, never
  2200          // user payloads, so they render unmasked like network-call telemetry.
  2201          const raw = line.written();
  2202          const visible = if (raw.len > trace_transcript_max_line_bytes)
  2203              raw[0..trace_transcript_max_line_bytes]
  2204          else
  2205              raw;
  2206          try writeTraceTextNeutralized(writer, visible);
  2207          if (raw.len > trace_transcript_max_line_bytes) {
  2208              try writer.writeAll(" ...\n");
  2209          } else {
  2210              try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2211          }
  2212      }
  2213  }
  2214  
  2215  /// Renders the retained session title generation outcome so a shared trace can
  2216  /// explain why a session has no generated title even when FX_TRACE was off.
  2217  fn writeSessionTitleSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer, app: anytype, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  2218      const App = @TypeOf(app.*);
  2219      if (comptime !agents.hasAgentField(App, "session_persistence")) return;
  2220      const Persistence = @TypeOf(agents.screenAgentOf(app).session_persistence);
  2221      if (comptime !@hasField(Persistence, "title_generation")) return;
  2222      const TitleGeneration = @TypeOf(agents.screenAgentOf(app).session_persistence.title_generation);
  2223      if (comptime !@hasField(TitleGeneration, "last")) return;
  2224  
  2225      try writer.writeAll("\n## Session Title\n");
  2226      if (comptime @hasField(App, "session_title_generation")) {
  2227          try writer.print("setting: {s}\n", .{boolLabel(app.session_title_generation)});
  2228      }
  2229      if (comptime agents.hasAgentField(App, "session_title")) {
  2230          if (agents.screenAgentOf(app).session_title.items.len > 0) {
  2231              try writer.writeAll("title: ");
  2232              try writeMaskedInline(writer, alloc, agents.screenAgentOf(app).session_title.items);
  2233              try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2234          } else {
  2235              try writer.writeAll("title: (none)\n");
  2236          }
  2237      }
  2238      const generation = &agents.screenAgentOf(app).session_persistence.title_generation;
  2239      if (generation.task) |task| {
  2240          try writer.print("generation: status=running session={s} model={s}", .{ task.session_id, task.model });
  2241          if (task.started_at_ms > 0) {
  2242              const elapsed = io_mod.milliTimestamp() - task.started_at_ms;
  2243              if (elapsed >= 0) try writer.print(" elapsed={d}ms", .{elapsed});
  2244          }
  2245          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2246          return;
  2247      }
  2248      const last = &generation.last;
  2249      try writer.print("generation: status={s}", .{@tagName(last.status)});
  2250      if (last.sessionId().len > 0) try writer.print(" session={s}", .{last.sessionId()});
  2251      if (last.model().len > 0) try writer.print(" model={s}", .{last.model()});
  2252      if (last.reason) |reason| try writer.print(" reason={s}", .{@tagName(reason)});
  2253      if (last.detail.len > 0) try writer.print(" detail={s}", .{last.detail});
  2254      if (last.elapsed_ms >= 0) try writer.print(" elapsed={d}ms", .{last.elapsed_ms});
  2255      try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2256  }
  2257  
  2258  /// The MCP servers, one line each; false when there is no host to ask.
  2259  fn writeMcpHostServers(writer: *std.Io.Writer, app: anytype, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !bool {
  2260      if (comptime !@hasDecl(@TypeOf(app.*), "mcpHostStatuses")) return false;
  2261      var arena = std.heap.ArenaAllocator.init(alloc);
  2262      defer arena.deinit();
  2263      const statuses = (try app.mcpHostStatuses(arena.allocator())) orelse return false;
  2264      if (statuses.len == 0) {
  2265          try writer.writeAll("mcp_servers: none\n");
  2266          return true;
  2267      }
  2268      try writer.print("mcp_servers ({d}):\n", .{statuses.len});
  2269      for (statuses) |s| {
  2270          try writer.print("  - {s} state={s} transport={s} tools={d} signed_in={s} needs_login={s}\n", .{
  2271              s.name,
  2272              @tagName(s.state),
  2273              @tagName(s.transport),
  2274              s.tools,
  2275              boolLabel(s.signed_in),
  2276              boolLabel(s.needs_login),
  2277          });
  2278          if (s.last_error) |err| {
  2279              try writer.writeAll("    error: ");
  2280              try writeMaskedInline(writer, alloc, err);
  2281              try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2282          }
  2283      }
  2284      return true;
  2285  }
  2286  
  2287  /// The MCP servers that need someone to act: down, retrying, missing a
  2288  /// variable, or waiting for a login.
  2289  fn writeMcpHostProblems(writer: *std.Io.Writer, app: anytype, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !usize {
  2290      if (comptime !@hasDecl(@TypeOf(app.*), "mcpHostStatuses")) return 0;
  2291      var arena = std.heap.ArenaAllocator.init(alloc);
  2292      defer arena.deinit();
  2293      const statuses = (try app.mcpHostStatuses(arena.allocator())) orelse return 0;
  2294      var count: usize = 0;
  2295      for (statuses) |s| {
  2296          const what = switch (s.state) {
  2297              .failed => "failed",
  2298              .retrying => "retrying",
  2299              .missing_env => "missing a variable",
  2300              .invalid_env => "can't use a variable",
  2301              else => if (s.needs_login) "needs login" else continue,
  2302          };
  2303          count += 1;
  2304          try writer.print("- mcp server {s} name={s}", .{ what, s.name });
  2305          if (s.missing) |variable| try writer.print(" variable={s}", .{variable});
  2306          if (s.last_error) |err| {
  2307              try writer.writeAll(" error=");
  2308              try writeMaskedInline(writer, alloc, err);
  2309          }
  2310          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2311      }
  2312      return count;
  2313  }

  2315  fn writeRuntimeContextSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer, app: anytype, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  2316      try writer.writeAll("\n## Runtime Context\n");
  2317      try writer.print("TERM: {s}\n", .{io_mod.getenv("TERM") orelse "(unset)"});
  2318      try writer.print("TERM_PROGRAM: {s}\n", .{io_mod.getenv("TERM_PROGRAM") orelse "(unset)"});
  2319      try writer.print("LANG: {s}\n", .{io_mod.getenv("LANG") orelse "(unset)"});
  2320      try writer.print("terminal_hosts: tmux={s} cmux={s}\n", .{
  2321          boolLabel(io_mod.getenv("TMUX") != null),
  2322          boolLabel(io_mod.getenv("CMUX_WORKSPACE_ID") != null),
  2323      });
  2324  
  2325      if (try writeMcpHostServers(writer, app, alloc)) return;
  2326      try writer.writeAll("mcp_servers: none\n");
  2327  }

  2329  fn writeRendererState(writer: *std.Io.Writer, app: anytype, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  2330      const transcript_commit = app.shell.transcriptCommitDiagnostic();
  2331      try writer.writeAll("\n## Renderer State\n");
  2332      try writer.print(
  2333          "layout: cols={d} rows={d} content_bottom={d} divider_top={d} input_row={d}\n",
  2334          .{ app.shell.layout.cols, app.shell.layout.rows, app.shell.layout.content_bottom, app.shell.layout.divider_top_row, app.shell.layout.input_row },
  2335      );
  2336      try writer.print(
  2337          "cursor: row={d} col={d} viewport_top={d} owned_top={d} last_visible_top={d} last_start_line={d} partial_skip={d}\n",
  2338          .{
  2339              app.shell.cursor_row,
  2340              app.shell.cursor_col,
  2341              app.shell.viewport_top_row,
  2342              app.shell.owned_top_row,
  2343              app.shell.last_visible_transcript_top_row,
  2344              app.shell.last_visible_transcript_start_line,
  2345              app.shell.last_visible_transcript_partial_skip_rows,
  2346          },
  2347      );
  2348      try writer.print(
  2349          "transcript: bytes={d} entries={d} last_rendered_cols={d} dirty={s} has_painted={s} commit_state={s} committed_start_line={d} pending_compact={s}\n",
  2350          .{
  2351              app.shell.notebook.transcript.items.len,
  2352              app.shell.notebook.entries.items.len,
  2353              app.shell.notebook.last_rendered_cols,
  2354              boolLabel(app.shell.transcript_band_dirty),
  2355              boolLabel(app.shell.has_painted_transcript),
  2356              @tagName(transcript_commit.state),
  2357              transcript_commit.stable_start_line orelse 0,
  2358              boolLabel(app.shell.pending_scroll_compact),
  2359          },
  2360      );
  2361      try writer.print(
  2362          "projection: view={d} history={d} total_rows={d} source_bytes={d} recovery={s} catchup={s}\n",
  2363          .{ transcript_commit.visual_offset, transcript_commit.history_visual_offset, transcript_commit.total_visual_rows, transcript_commit.source_bytes, boolLabel(app.shell.normalBufferRecoveryPending()), boolLabel(app.shell.historyCatchupPending()) },
  2364      );
  2365      try writer.print(
  2366          "replaceable: active={s} row={d} start={d}\n",
  2367          .{ boolLabel(app.shell.notebook.replaceable_last_line), app.shell.replaceable_row, app.shell.notebook.replaceable_start },
  2368      );
  2369  
  2370      const grid = app.shell.shadow_vt orelse {
  2371          try writer.writeAll("shadow_grid: unavailable\n");
  2372          return;
  2373      };
  2374      try writer.print(
  2375          "shadow_grid: cols={d} rows={d} cursor={d},{d} visible={s} sync_active={s}\n",
  2376          .{ grid.cols, grid.rows, grid.cursor_row, grid.cursor_col, boolLabel(grid.cursor_visible), boolLabel(grid.sync_active) },
  2377      );
  2378      try writer.writeAll("shadow_grid_visible_rows:\n");
  2379      var emitted_any = false;
  2380      var row: u16 = 1;
  2381      while (row <= grid.rows) : (row += 1) {
  2382          var row_text: std.ArrayList(u8) = .empty;
  2383          defer row_text.deinit(alloc);
  2384          try grid.rowTextTrimmed(row, &row_text);
  2385          const trimmed = std.mem.trimEnd(u8, row_text.items, " \t\r");
  2386          if (trimmed.len == 0) continue;
  2387          emitted_any = true;
  2388          const masked = try text_utils.maskSecrets(alloc, trimmed);
  2389          defer if (masked.ptr != trimmed.ptr) alloc.free(masked);
  2390          try writer.print("  {d:0>3}: {s}\n", .{ row, masked });
  2391      }
  2392      if (!emitted_any) try writer.writeAll("  (empty)\n");
  2393  }

  2395  fn writeRendererEvents(writer: *std.Io.Writer, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  2396      var events: [diagnostics.render_ring_capacity]diagnostics.RenderEvent = undefined;
  2397      const count = diagnostics.snapshotRenderEvents(&events);
  2398      try writer.writeAll("\n## Recent Renderer Events\n");
  2399      try writer.writeAll("Internal rendering decisions, not terminal readback. Idle and same-row updates are omitted.\n");
  2400      if (count == 0) {
  2401          try writer.writeAll("(none recorded)\n");
  2402          return;
  2403      }
  2404      try writer.print("retained={d} capacity={d} overwritten_before={d}\n", .{
  2405          count, diagnostics.render_ring_capacity, events[0].sequence -| 1,
  2406      });
  2407      for (events[0..count]) |*event| {
  2408          try writeTraceTimestampUtc(writer, event.timestamp_ms);
  2409          try writer.print(" seq={d} kind={s} ", .{ event.sequence, @tagName(event.kind) });
  2410          try writeMaskedInline(writer, alloc, event.detail());
  2411          if (event.truncated) try writer.writeAll(" [truncated]");
  2412          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2413      }
  2414  }

  2416  test "trace renderer events are available without opt-in file logging" {
  2417      diagnostics.resetForTest();
  2418      defer diagnostics.resetForTest();
  2419      debug_trace.shutdown();
  2420      diagnostics.recordRenderEvent(.transition, "release_past_finality history={d} releasable={d}", .{ 21, 20 });
  2421      var out: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = .init(std.testing.allocator);
  2422      defer out.deinit();
  2423      try writeRendererEvents(&out.writer, std.testing.allocator);
  2424      try std.testing.expect(debug_trace.activeLogPath() == null);
  2425      try std.testing.expect(std.mem.find(u8, out.written(), "## Recent Renderer Events") != null);
  2426      try std.testing.expect(std.mem.find(u8, out.written(), "seq=1 kind=transition release_past_finality history=21 releasable=20") != null);
  2427      try std.testing.expect(std.mem.find(u8, out.written(), "overwritten_before=0") != null);
  2428  }

  2430  fn boolLabel(value: bool) []const u8 {
  2431      return if (value) "true" else "false";
  2432  }

  2434  fn lastInterruptedTurn(items: []const types.HistoryTurn) ?types.InterruptedHistoryTurn {
  2435      if (items.len == 0) return null;
  2436      return switch (items[items.len - 1]) {
  2437          .interrupted => |entry| entry,
  2438          else => null,
  2439      };
  2440  }

  2442  const trace_tool_args_max_bytes: usize = 1200;
  2443  
  2444  fn networkCallIsError(call: diagnostics.NetworkCall) bool {
  2445      return call.isError();
  2446  }
  2447  
  2448  fn writeNetworkCallCompact(writer: *std.Io.Writer, call: diagnostics.NetworkCall) !void {
  2449      try writeTraceTimestampUtc(writer, call.started_at_ms);
  2450      try writer.print(" model={s}", .{call.model()});
  2451      if (call.kind != .gateway) try writer.print(" kind={s}", .{@tagName(call.kind)});
  2452      if (call.subagent_id != 0) try writer.print(" source=subagent#{d}", .{call.subagent_id}) else try writer.writeAll(" source=parent");
  2453      if (call.errorName().len > 0) {
  2454          try writer.print(" err={s}", .{call.errorName()});
  2455      } else if (call.status != 0) {
  2456          try writer.print(" status={d}", .{call.status});
  2457      }
  2458      try writer.print(" duration={d}ms bytes={d}", .{ call.duration_ms, call.response_bytes });
  2459      if (call.input_tokens > 0 or call.output_tokens > 0) try writer.print(" tokens={d}->{d}", .{ call.input_tokens, call.output_tokens });
  2460      if (call.is_web_search) try writer.print(" web_search_requests={d}", .{call.web_search_requests});
  2461      if (call.terminalStopReason().len > 0) try writer.print(" stop_reason={s}", .{call.terminalStopReason()});
  2462      if (call.providerStopReason().len > 0) try writer.print(" provider_stop_reason={s}", .{call.providerStopReason()});
  2463      if (call.turn_id != 0) try writer.print(" turn={d}", .{call.turn_id});
  2464      if (call.step_id != 0) try writer.print(" step={d}", .{call.step_id});
  2465      if (call.gatewaySchemaDiagnostic().len > 0) try writer.print(" gateway_schema=\"{s}\"", .{call.gatewaySchemaDiagnostic()});
  2466      if (call.gatewayRequestShape().len > 0) try writer.print(" request_shape=\"{s}\"", .{call.gatewayRequestShape()});
  2467      try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2468  }
  2469  
  2470  fn writeNetworkCallsSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer) !void {
  2471      var buf: [diagnostics.network_ring_capacity]diagnostics.NetworkCall = undefined;
  2472      const n = diagnostics.snapshotNetworkCalls(&buf);
  2473      if (n == 0) {
  2474          try writer.writeAll("\n## Network Calls\n(none recorded)\n");
  2475          return;
  2476      }
  2477  
  2478      var ok_count: u32 = 0;
  2479      var error_count: u32 = 0;
  2480      var total_ms: u64 = 0;
  2481      var max_ms: u32 = 0;
  2482      var min_ms: u32 = std.math.maxInt(u32);
  2483      for (buf[0..n]) |call| {
  2484          if (networkCallIsError(call)) error_count += 1 else ok_count += 1;
  2485          total_ms += call.duration_ms;
  2486          if (call.duration_ms > max_ms) max_ms = call.duration_ms;
  2487          if (call.duration_ms < min_ms) min_ms = call.duration_ms;
  2488      }
  2489      const avg_ms: u64 = if (n > 0) total_ms / n else 0;
  2490  
  2491      try writer.print("\n## Network Calls\nlast={d} ok={d} errors={d} avg={d}ms min={d}ms max={d}ms\n", .{ n, ok_count, error_count, avg_ms, min_ms, max_ms });
  2492      try writeNetworkSessionTotals(writer, buf[0..n]);
  2493      for (buf[0..n]) |call| {
  2494          try writeNetworkCallCompact(writer, call);
  2495      }
  2496  }
  2497  
  2498  /// Renders session-wide totals, the per-turn model-call rollup, and an
  2499  /// explicit coverage statement so a shared trace cannot be misread as
  2500  /// describing the whole session when the ring has already evicted history.
  2501  fn writeNetworkSessionTotals(writer: *std.Io.Writer, window: []const diagnostics.NetworkCall) !void {
  2502      const lifetime = diagnostics.networkLifetimeStats();
  2503      try writer.print(
  2504          "session: calls={d} ok={d} errors={d} total_time={d}s\n",
  2505          .{ lifetime.total_calls, lifetime.ok_calls, lifetime.error_calls, lifetime.total_duration_ms / 1000 },
  2506      );
  2507      if (window.len > 0 and lifetime.total_calls > window.len) {
  2508          try writer.print(
  2509              "coverage: window holds only the last {d} of {d} calls, oldest retained ",
  2510              .{ window.len, lifetime.total_calls },
  2511          );
  2512          try writeTraceTimestampUtc(writer, window[0].started_at_ms);
  2513          try writer.writeAll("; run with FX_TRACE_LOG for a complete transport record\n");
  2514      } else {
  2515          try writer.writeAll("coverage: complete (window holds every recorded call)\n");
  2516      }
  2517  
  2518      var rollups: [diagnostics.network_turn_rollup_capacity]diagnostics.NetworkTurnRollup = undefined;
  2519      const rollup_n = diagnostics.snapshotNetworkTurnRollups(&rollups);
  2520      if (rollup_n == 0) return;
  2521      if (lifetime.evicted_turns > 0) {
  2522          try writer.print("turns: most recent {d} shown, {d} older evicted\n", .{ rollup_n, lifetime.evicted_turns });
  2523      } else {
  2524          try writer.writeAll("turns:\n");
  2525      }
  2526      for (rollups[0..rollup_n]) |rollup| {
  2527          try writer.print(
  2528              "  turn {d}: calls={d} errors={d} total_time={d}s",
  2529              .{ rollup.turn_id, rollup.calls, rollup.error_calls, rollup.total_duration_ms / 1000 },
  2530          );
  2531          if (rollup.subagent_calls > 0) try writer.print(" subagent_calls={d}", .{rollup.subagent_calls});
  2532          if (rollup.first_started_at_ms > 0) {
  2533              try writer.writeAll(" started ");
  2534              try writeTraceTimestampUtc(writer, rollup.first_started_at_ms);
  2535          }
  2536          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2537      }
  2538  }
  2539  
  2540  /// Renders catalog cache state, the selected model's resolved capabilities,
  2541  /// and the always-on catalog event ring so a shared trace explains why an
  2542  /// image submission or capability-dependent feature was rejected.
  2543  fn writeModelCatalogSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer, app: anytype) !void {
  2544      const App = @TypeOf(app.*);
  2545      try writer.writeAll("\n## Model Catalog\n");
  2546      if (comptime @hasField(App, "model_cache")) {
  2547          const snapshot = app.model_cache.snapshotForTrace();
  2548          try writer.print("state={s} entries={d}", .{ snapshot.state, snapshot.entries });
  2549          if (snapshot.last_attempt_age_ms) |age_ms| {
  2550              try writer.print(" last_attempt={d}s ago", .{@divFloor(@max(age_ms, 0), @as(i64, 1000))});
  2551          }
  2552          if (snapshot.failure_category) |category| {
  2553              try writer.print(" failure={s} retryable={s}", .{ category, boolLabel(snapshot.failure_retryable) });
  2554              if (snapshot.failure_http_status) |status| try writer.print(" status={d}", .{@backingInt(status)});
  2555              if (snapshot.anonymous_fallback) try writer.writeAll(" anonymous_fallback=true");
  2556          }
  2557          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2558      }
  2559      if (comptime @hasDecl(App, "resolvedModelCapabilities")) {
  2560          const model = provider_runtime.model(app);
  2561          const capabilities = app.resolvedModelCapabilities(model);
  2562          try writer.print("selected_model={s} image_input={s} vision={s} file_input={s} tool_use={s}", .{
  2563              model,
  2564              @tagName(capabilities.image_input_support),
  2565              boolLabel(capabilities.supports_vision),
  2566              boolLabel(capabilities.supports_file_input),
  2567              boolLabel(capabilities.supports_tool_use),
  2568          });
  2569          if (capabilities.context_window) |window| try writer.print(" context_window={d}", .{window});
  2570          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2571      }
  2572      var buf: [diagnostics.model_catalog_ring_capacity]diagnostics.ModelCatalogEvent = undefined;
  2573      const total = diagnostics.snapshotModelCatalogEvents(&buf);
  2574      if (total == 0) {
  2575          try writer.writeAll("(no catalog events recorded)\n");
  2576          return;
  2577      }
  2578      try writer.print("events={d} (always recorded; does not require FX_TRACE)\n", .{total});
  2579      for (buf[0..total]) |*event| {
  2580          try writeTraceTimestampUtc(writer, event.timestamp_ms);
  2581          try writer.print(" seq={d} {s}", .{ event.sequence, event.name() });
  2582          if (event.failed) try writer.writeAll(" failed");
  2583          if (event.detail().len > 0) {
  2584              try writer.writeByte(' ');
  2585              try writer.writeAll(event.detail());
  2586          }
  2587          if (event.truncated) try writer.writeAll(" [truncated]");
  2588          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2589      }
  2590  }
  2591  
  2592  fn writePermissionsSummary(writer: *std.Io.Writer, grants: []const types.PermissionGrant) !void {
  2593      if (grants.len == 0) {
  2594          try writer.writeAll("\n## Permissions\npermission grants: none\n");
  2595          return;
  2596      }
  2597      try writer.print("\n## Permissions\npermission grants ({d}):\n", .{grants.len});
  2598      for (grants) |grant| {
  2599          try writer.print("  - {s} :: {s}\n", .{ traceToolDisplayName(grant.tool_name), grant.target_path });
  2600      }
  2601  }

  2603  fn writeToolCallCompact(writer: *std.Io.Writer, call: diagnostics.ToolCallMetric) !void {
  2604      try writeTraceTimestampUtc(writer, call.started_at_ms);
  2605      try writer.print(" name={s} outcome={s} duration={d}ms", .{ traceToolDisplayName(call.name()), @tagName(call.outcome), call.duration_ms });
  2606      if (call.subagent_id != 0) try writer.print(" source=subagent#{d}", .{call.subagent_id}) else try writer.writeAll(" source=parent");
  2607      try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2608  }

  2610  const ProviderToolCallSummary = struct {
  2611      call_id: []const u8,
  2612      status: ?types.PersistedToolStatus,
  2613  };
  2614  
  2615  fn findPersistedToolResult(
  2616      execution: types.ExecutionMemory,
  2617      call_id: []const u8,
  2618  ) ?types.PersistedToolResult {
  2619      for (execution.tool_steps) |step| {
  2620          for (step.tool_results) |result| {
  2621              if (std.mem.eql(u8, result.tool_call_id, call_id)) return result;
  2622          }
  2623      }
  2624      return null;
  2625  }
  2626  
  2627  fn projectProviderToolCalls(
  2628      history: []const types.HistoryTurn,
  2629      output: []ProviderToolCallSummary,
  2630  ) usize {
  2631      if (output.len == 0) return 0;
  2632  
  2633      var count: usize = 0;
  2634      for (history) |turn| {
  2635          const execution: types.ExecutionMemory = switch (turn) {
  2636              .assistant => |entry| entry.execution,
  2637              .interrupted => |entry| entry.execution,
  2638              .compacted_summary => continue,
  2639          };
  2640          for (execution.tool_steps) |step| {
  2641              for (step.tool_calls) |call| {
  2642                  if (call.provenance != .provider_executed or
  2643                      !tool_presentation.isProviderSearchAlias(call.name)) continue;
  2644                  const summary: ProviderToolCallSummary = .{
  2645                      .call_id = call.id,
  2646                      .status = if (findPersistedToolResult(execution, call.id)) |result|
  2647                          result.status
  2648                      else
  2649                          null,
  2650                  };
  2651                  if (count < output.len) {
  2652                      output[count] = summary;
  2653                      count += 1;
  2654                  } else {
  2655                      @memmove(
  2656                          output[0 .. output.len - 1],
  2657                          output[1..],
  2658                      );
  2659                      output[output.len - 1] = summary;
  2660                  }
  2661              }
  2662          }
  2663      }
  2664      return count;
  2665  }
  2666  
  2667  fn providerToolStatusLabel(status: ?types.PersistedToolStatus) []const u8 {
  2668      return if (status) |value| switch (value) {
  2669          .success => "ok",
  2670          .failure => "err",
  2671      } else "pending";
  2672  }
  2673  
  2674  noinline fn writeToolCallsSummary(
  2675      writer: *std.Io.Writer,
  2676      alloc: std.mem.Allocator,
  2677      history: []const types.HistoryTurn,
  2678  ) !void {
  2679      var buf: [diagnostics.tool_call_ring_capacity]diagnostics.ToolCallMetric = undefined;
  2680      const n = diagnostics.snapshotToolCalls(&buf);
  2681      var provider_buf: [diagnostics.tool_call_ring_capacity]ProviderToolCallSummary = undefined;
  2682      const provider_n = projectProviderToolCalls(history, &provider_buf);
  2683      if (n == 0 and provider_n == 0) {
  2684          try writer.writeAll("\n## Tool Calls\n(none recorded)\n");
  2685          return;
  2686      }
  2687  
  2688      try writer.writeAll("\n## Tool Calls\n### Local\n");
  2689      if (n == 0) {
  2690          try writer.writeAll("(none locally executed)\n");
  2691      } else {
  2692          var succeeded_count: u32 = 0;
  2693          var rejected_count: u32 = 0;
  2694          var command_failed_count: u32 = 0;
  2695          var tool_failed_count: u32 = 0;
  2696          var runtime_failed_count: u32 = 0;
  2697          var total_ms: u64 = 0;
  2698          for (buf[0..n]) |call| {
  2699              switch (call.outcome) {
  2700                  .succeeded => succeeded_count += 1,
  2701                  .rejected => rejected_count += 1,
  2702                  .command_failed => command_failed_count += 1,
  2703                  .tool_failed => tool_failed_count += 1,
  2704                  .runtime_failed => runtime_failed_count += 1,
  2705              }
  2706              total_ms += call.duration_ms;
  2707          }
  2708  
  2709          try writer.print(
  2710              "last={d} succeeded={d} rejected={d} command_failed={d} tool_failed={d} runtime_failed={d} total={d}ms\n",
  2711              .{ n, succeeded_count, rejected_count, command_failed_count, tool_failed_count, runtime_failed_count, total_ms },
  2712          );
  2713          const lifetime = diagnostics.toolCallLifetimeStats();
  2714          try writer.print(
  2715              "session: calls={d} succeeded={d} rejected={d} command_failed={d} tool_failed={d} runtime_failed={d} total_time={d}s\n",
  2716              .{
  2717                  lifetime.total_calls,
  2718                  lifetime.countFor(.succeeded),
  2719                  lifetime.countFor(.rejected),
  2720                  lifetime.countFor(.command_failed),
  2721                  lifetime.countFor(.tool_failed),
  2722                  lifetime.countFor(.runtime_failed),
  2723                  lifetime.total_duration_ms / 1000,
  2724              },
  2725          );
  2726          if (lifetime.total_calls > n) {
  2727              try writer.print(
  2728                  "coverage: window holds only the last {d} of {d} tool calls; full results persist in the session directory\n",
  2729                  .{ n, lifetime.total_calls },
  2730              );
  2731          } else {
  2732              try writer.writeAll("coverage: complete (window holds every recorded tool call)\n");
  2733          }
  2734          if (succeeded_count != n) {
  2735              try writer.writeAll("non-successes first:\n");
  2736              for (buf[0..n]) |call| {
  2737                  if (call.outcome == .succeeded) continue;
  2738                  try writeToolCallCompact(writer, call);
  2739                  try writeToolFieldBlock(writer, alloc, "args", call.args(), call.args_len, call.args_total_bytes);
  2740                  try writeToolFieldBlock(writer, alloc, "result", call.result(), call.result_len, call.result_total_bytes);
  2741              }
  2742              try writer.writeAll("recent successes (compact):\n");
  2743          } else {
  2744              try writer.writeAll("recent successes (compact):\n");
  2745          }
  2746          for (buf[0..n]) |call| {
  2747              if (call.outcome != .succeeded) continue;
  2748              try writeToolCallCompact(writer, call);
  2749              try writeToolFieldBlock(writer, alloc, "args", call.args(), call.args_len, call.args_total_bytes);
  2750              try writeToolResultPreview(writer, alloc, call.result(), call.result_total_bytes);
  2751          }
  2752      }
  2753  
  2754      try writer.writeAll("### Web Search\n");
  2755      if (provider_n == 0) {
  2756          try writer.writeAll("(none retained)\n");
  2757          return;
  2758      }
  2759      try writer.print("last={d}\n", .{provider_n});
  2760      for (provider_buf[0..provider_n]) |call| {
  2761          try writer.print(
  2762              "name=web_search status={s}\n",
  2763              .{providerToolStatusLabel(call.status)},
  2764          );
  2765      }
  2766  }
  2767  
  2768  fn writeToolResultPreview(writer: *std.Io.Writer, alloc: std.mem.Allocator, body: []const u8, total_bytes: u32) !void {
  2769      if (total_bytes == 0) return;
  2770      const raw_trimmed = std.mem.trim(u8, body, " \t\r\n");
  2771      const masked = try text_utils.maskSecrets(alloc, raw_trimmed);
  2772      defer if (masked.ptr != raw_trimmed.ptr) alloc.free(masked);
  2773      const trimmed = std.mem.trim(u8, masked, " \t\r\n");
  2774      if (trimmed.len == 0) return;
  2775  
  2776      const preview = firstUsefulPreviewLine(trimmed);
  2777      try writer.writeAll("  result_preview: ");
  2778      if (preview.len > 180) {
  2779          try writer.writeAll(preview[0..180]);
  2780          try writer.writeAll(" ...");
  2781      } else {
  2782          try writer.writeAll(preview);
  2783      }
  2784      if (total_bytes > preview.len) try writer.print(" ({d} bytes total)", .{total_bytes});
  2785      try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2786  }
  2787  
  2788  fn firstUsefulPreviewLine(text: []const u8) []const u8 {
  2789      var it = std.mem.splitScalar(u8, text, '\n');
  2790      while (it.next()) |line| {
  2791          const trimmed = std.mem.trim(u8, line, " \t\r");
  2792          if (trimmed.len == 0) continue;
  2793          if (std.mem.eql(u8, trimmed, ":")) continue;
  2794          return trimmed;
  2795      }
  2796      return text[0..@min(text.len, 180)];
  2797  }
  2798  
  2799  fn writeToolFieldBlock(writer: *std.Io.Writer, alloc: std.mem.Allocator, label: []const u8, body: []const u8, body_len: u16, total_bytes: u32) !void {
  2800      if (total_bytes == 0) return;
  2801      const raw_trimmed = std.mem.trim(u8, body, " \t\r\n");
  2802      const masked = try text_utils.maskSecrets(alloc, raw_trimmed);
  2803      defer if (masked.ptr != raw_trimmed.ptr) alloc.free(masked);
  2804      const trimmed = std.mem.trim(u8, masked, " \t\r\n");
  2805      if (trimmed.len == 0) return;
  2806  
  2807      if (std.mem.findScalar(u8, trimmed, '\n') == null) {
  2808          try writer.print("  {s}: {s}", .{ label, trimmed });
  2809          if (total_bytes > body_len) {
  2810              try writer.print(" ... ({d} more bytes)", .{total_bytes - body_len});
  2811          }
  2812          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2813          return;
  2814      }
  2815  
  2816      try writer.print("  {s}:\n", .{label});
  2817      var it = std.mem.splitScalar(u8, trimmed, '\n');
  2818      while (it.next()) |line| {
  2819          try writer.print("    {s}\n", .{std.mem.trimEnd(u8, line, " \t\r")});
  2820      }
  2821      if (total_bytes > body_len) {
  2822          try writer.print("    ... ({d} more bytes)\n", .{total_bytes - body_len});
  2823      }
  2824  }
  2825  
  2826  fn writeLastInterruptedDetail(writer: *std.Io.Writer, items: []const types.HistoryTurn, alloc: std.mem.Allocator) !void {
  2827      if (items.len == 0) return;
  2828      const last = items[items.len - 1];
  2829      switch (last) {
  2830          .interrupted => |t| {
  2831              try writer.writeAll("\n## Interrupted Turn\nlast turn was interrupted by user\n");
  2832              if (t.tool_call) |call| {
  2833                  try writer.print("  in_flight_tool: {s}\n", .{traceToolDisplayName(call.name)});
  2834                  if (call.arguments_json.len > 0) {
  2835                      try writer.writeAll("  in_flight_args:\n");
  2836                      if (call.arguments_json.len > trace_tool_args_max_bytes) {
  2837                          try writer.writeAll("    ");
  2838                          try writeMaskedInline(writer, alloc, call.arguments_json[0..trace_tool_args_max_bytes]);
  2839                          try writer.print("\n    ... (truncated, {d} more bytes)\n", .{call.arguments_json.len - trace_tool_args_max_bytes});
  2840                      } else {
  2841                          try writer.writeAll("    ");
  2842                          try writeMaskedInline(writer, alloc, call.arguments_json);
  2843                          try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2844                      }
  2845                  }
  2846              }
  2847              if (t.completed_tool_names.len > 0) {
  2848                  try writer.print("  completed_tools ({d}):", .{t.completed_tool_names.len});
  2849                  for (t.completed_tool_names) |name| try writer.print(" {s}", .{traceToolDisplayName(name)});
  2850                  try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2851              }
  2852          },
  2853          else => {},
  2854      }
  2855  }
  2856  
  2857  const trace_tail_bytes: usize = 6 * 1024;
  2858  const trace_max_lines: usize = 80;
  2859  
  2860  fn writeTraceLogTail(writer: *std.Io.Writer, alloc: std.mem.Allocator, path: []const u8) !void {
  2861      var file = std.Io.Dir.openFileAbsolute(io_mod.getIo(), path, .{}) catch return;
  2862      defer file.close(io_mod.getIo());
  2863  
  2864      var stat_buf: std.Io.File.Stat = undefined;
  2865      stat_buf = file.stat(io_mod.getIo()) catch return;
  2866      const total_size: usize = @intCast(stat_buf.size);
  2867      if (total_size == 0) return;
  2868  
  2869      const read_size = @min(total_size, trace_tail_bytes);
  2870      const offset: u64 = total_size - read_size;
  2871  
  2872      const buf = alloc.alloc(u8, read_size) catch return;
  2873      defer alloc.free(buf);
  2874      const n = file.readPositionalAll(io_mod.getIo(), buf, offset) catch return;
  2875      if (n == 0) return;
  2876  
  2877      try writer.print("\n## Trace Tail\npath={s} last_bytes={d}\n", .{ path, n });
  2878      try writer.writeAll("only obvious secrets masked\n");
  2879      if (offset > 0) try writer.writeAll("... (older lines truncated)\n");
  2880  
  2881      var lines: std.ArrayList([]const u8) = .empty;
  2882      defer lines.deinit(alloc);
  2883      var it = std.mem.splitScalar(u8, buf[0..n], '\n');
  2884      while (it.next()) |line| {
  2885          const trimmed = std.mem.trimEnd(u8, line, " \t\r");
  2886          if (trimmed.len == 0) continue;
  2887          try lines.append(alloc, trimmed);
  2888      }
  2889  
  2890      const start = if (lines.items.len > trace_max_lines)
  2891          lines.items.len - trace_max_lines
  2892      else
  2893          0;
  2894  
  2895      for (lines.items[start..]) |line| {
  2896          const masked = try text_utils.maskSecrets(alloc, line);
  2897          defer if (masked.ptr != line.ptr) alloc.free(masked);
  2898          const visible = if (masked.len > trace_transcript_max_line_bytes)
  2899              masked[0..trace_transcript_max_line_bytes]
  2900          else
  2901              masked;
  2902          try writeTraceTextNeutralized(writer, visible);
  2903          if (masked.len > trace_transcript_max_line_bytes) {
  2904              try writer.writeAll(" ...\n");
  2905          } else {
  2906              try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2907          }
  2908      }
  2909  }
  2910  
  2911  fn writeTraceTimestampUtc(writer: *std.Io.Writer, ms: i64) !void {
  2912      if (ms <= 0) {
  2913          try writer.writeAll("[----------T--:--:--Z]");
  2914          return;
  2915      }
  2916      const secs: u64 = @intCast(@divFloor(ms, 1000));
  2917      const ms_part: u64 = @intCast(@mod(ms, 1000));
  2918      const epoch_secs: std.time.epoch.EpochSeconds = .{ .secs = @intCast(secs) };
  2919      const day_seconds = epoch_secs.getDaySeconds();
  2920      const year_day = epoch_secs.getEpochDay().calculateYearDay();
  2921      const month_day = year_day.calculateMonthDay();
  2922      try writer.print("[{d}-{d:0>2}-{d:0>2}T{d:0>2}:{d:0>2}:{d:0>2}.{d:0>3}Z]", .{
  2923          year_day.year,
  2924          month_day.month.numeric(),
  2925          month_day.day_index + 1,
  2926          day_seconds.getHoursIntoDay(),
  2927          day_seconds.getMinutesIntoHour(),
  2928          day_seconds.getSecondsIntoMinute(),
  2929          ms_part,
  2930      });
  2931  }
  2932  
  2933  noinline fn writeTranscriptTimeline(writer: *std.Io.Writer, alloc: std.mem.Allocator, entries: anytype) !void {
  2934      var emitted_any = false;
  2935      for (entries) |entry| {
  2936          const ts = entry.createdAtMs();
  2937          var rendered_payload: ?[]u8 = null;
  2938          defer if (rendered_payload) |text| alloc.free(text);
  2939          const kind: []const u8 = switch (entry) {
  2940              .raw_bytes => "raw",
  2941              .user_turn => "user",
  2942              .assistant_turn => "assistant",
  2943              .assistant_table => "assistant table",
  2944              .assistant_code_block => "assistant code block",
  2945              .assistant_thematic_rule => "assistant thematic rule",
  2946              .semantic_notice => "semantic notice",
  2947          };
  2948          const text: []const u8 = switch (entry) {
  2949              .raw_bytes => |e| e.bytes,
  2950              .user_turn => |e| e.turn.text,
  2951              .assistant_turn => |e| e.segments.text.items,
  2952              .assistant_table => |e| blk: {
  2953                  var table_text: std.ArrayList(u8) = .empty;
  2954                  errdefer table_text.deinit(alloc);
  2955                  try assistant_presentation.renderTablePayload(alloc, e.table, &table_text);
  2956                  rendered_payload = try table_text.toOwnedSlice(alloc);
  2957                  break :blk rendered_payload.?;
  2958              },
  2959              .assistant_code_block => |e| blk: {
  2960                  var code_text: std.ArrayList(u8) = .empty;
  2961                  errdefer code_text.deinit(alloc);
  2962                  try assistant_presentation.renderCodeBlockPayload(alloc, e.block, &code_text);
  2963                  rendered_payload = try code_text.toOwnedSlice(alloc);
  2964                  break :blk rendered_payload.?;
  2965              },
  2966              .assistant_thematic_rule => blk: {
  2967                  var rule_text: std.ArrayList(u8) = .empty;
  2968                  errdefer rule_text.deinit(alloc);
  2969                  try assistant_presentation.writeHorizontalRule(alloc, &rule_text);
  2970                  rendered_payload = try rule_text.toOwnedSlice(alloc);
  2971                  break :blk rendered_payload.?;
  2972              },
  2973              .semantic_notice => |e| blk: {
  2974                  const rendered = try transcript_blocks.renderSemanticNotice(
  2975                      alloc,
  2976                      .{
  2977                          .topic = e.topic,
  2978                          .tone = e.tone,
  2979                          .body = e.body,
  2980                          .visibility = e.visibility,
  2981                      },
  2982                      .{},
  2983                      std.math.maxInt(u16),
  2984                  );
  2985                  rendered_payload = rendered;
  2986                  break :blk rendered;
  2987              },
  2988          };
  2989          if (text.len == 0) continue;
  2990  
  2991          const body = switch (entry) {
  2992              .assistant_thematic_rule => try renderThematicRuleTimelineBody(alloc, text),
  2993              else => try renderTimelineEntryBody(alloc, text),
  2994          };
  2995          defer alloc.free(body);
  2996          if (body.len == 0) continue;
  2997  
  2998          if (emitted_any) try writer.writeByte('\n');
  2999          try writeTraceTimestampUtc(writer, ts);
  3000          try writer.print(" {s}\n", .{kind});
  3001          try writer.writeAll(body);
  3002          emitted_any = true;
  3003      }
  3004      if (!emitted_any) try writer.writeAll("(empty after filtering)\n");
  3005  }
  3006  
  3007  fn renderTimelineEntryBody(alloc: std.mem.Allocator, text: []const u8) ![]u8 {
  3008      const stripped = try stripAnsiEscapes(alloc, text);
  3009      defer alloc.free(stripped);
  3010  
  3011      var out: std.ArrayList(u8) = .empty;
  3012      errdefer out.deinit(alloc);
  3013  
  3014      var it = std.mem.splitScalar(u8, stripped, '\n');
  3015      while (it.next()) |line| {
  3016          const trimmed = std.mem.trimEnd(u8, line, " \t\r");
  3017          if (trimmed.len == 0) continue;
  3018          if (isBannerNoiseLine(trimmed)) continue;
  3019          const masked = try text_utils.maskSecrets(alloc, trimmed);
  3020          defer if (masked.ptr != trimmed.ptr) alloc.free(masked);
  3021          try out.appendSlice(alloc, "  ");
  3022          try out.appendSlice(alloc, masked);
  3023          try out.append(alloc, '\n');
  3024      }
  3025      return out.toOwnedSlice(alloc);
  3026  }
  3027  
  3028  fn renderThematicRuleTimelineBody(alloc: std.mem.Allocator, text: []const u8) ![]u8 {
  3029      const stripped = try stripAnsiEscapes(alloc, text);
  3030      defer alloc.free(stripped);
  3031      const trimmed = std.mem.trimEnd(u8, stripped, " \t\r\n");
  3032      return alloc.print("  {s}\n", .{trimmed});
  3033  }

  3968      diagnostics.resetForTest();
  3969      for (0..diagnostics.compaction_ring_capacity + 3) |index| {
  3970          compactor.traceEvent(.{ .turn_id = 11 }, .decision, "decision=compact index={d}", .{index});
  3971      }
  3972      var wrapped: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = .init(alloc);
  3973      defer wrapped.deinit();
  3974      try writeCompactionSummary(&wrapped.writer, alloc);
  3975      try std.testing.expect(std.mem.find(u8, wrapped.written(), "overwritten_before=3") != null);
  3976  }

  4013  test "trace model catalog summary renders cache state and failure detail" {
  4014      const alloc = std.testing.allocator;
  4015      diagnostics.resetForTest();
  4016      defer diagnostics.resetForTest();
  4017  
  4018      const Snapshot = struct {
  4019          state: []const u8,
  4020          entries: usize,
  4021          last_attempt_age_ms: ?i64,
  4022          failure_category: ?[]const u8,
  4023          failure_http_status: ?std.http.Status,
  4024          failure_retryable: bool,
  4025          anonymous_fallback: bool,
  4026      };
  4027      const CacheStub = struct {
  4028          snapshot: Snapshot,
  4029          fn snapshotForTrace(self: *@This()) Snapshot {
  4030              return self.snapshot;
  4031          }
  4032      };
  4033      const StubApp = struct {
  4034          model_cache: CacheStub,
  4035      };
  4036  
  4037      var stub: StubApp = .{ .model_cache = .{ .snapshot = .{
  4038          .state = "failed",
  4039          .entries = 0,
  4040          .last_attempt_age_ms = null,
  4041          .failure_category = "transport",
  4042          .failure_http_status = .service_unavailable,
  4043          .failure_retryable = true,
  4044          .anonymous_fallback = true,
  4045      } } };
  4046      var failed: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = .init(alloc);
  4047      defer failed.deinit();
  4048      try writeModelCatalogSummary(&failed.writer, &stub);
  4049      try std.testing.expect(std.mem.find(u8, failed.written(), "state=failed entries=0 failure=transport retryable=true status=503 anonymous_fallback=true\n") != null);
  4050  
  4051      stub.model_cache.snapshot = .{
  4052          .state = "ready",
  4053          .entries = 41,
  4054          .last_attempt_age_ms = null,
  4055          .failure_category = null,
  4056          .failure_http_status = null,
  4057          .failure_retryable = false,
  4058          .anonymous_fallback = false,
  4059      };
  4060      var ready: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = .init(alloc);
  4061      defer ready.deinit();
  4062      try writeModelCatalogSummary(&ready.writer, &stub);
  4063      try std.testing.expect(std.mem.find(u8, ready.written(), "state=ready entries=41\n") != null);
  4064  
  4065      stub.model_cache.snapshot.last_attempt_age_ms = 12_500;
  4066      var aged: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = .init(alloc);
  4067      defer aged.deinit();
  4068      try writeModelCatalogSummary(&aged.writer, &stub);
  4069      try std.testing.expect(std.mem.find(u8, aged.written(), "state=ready entries=41 last_attempt=12s ago\n") != null);
  4070  }

  4123              session_persistence: app_session_runtime.Persistence = .{},

  4125          } = .{},
  4126          session_title_generation: bool = true,
  4127
